Optimize lookup in AbandonedPageLoadMetricsObserver

Replaces GetCustomUserTimingMarkNames() with
GetCustomUserTimingMarkMilestone(), allowing subclasses to look up a
milestone directly for a given mark name instead of exposing an entire
map.

In GWSAbandonedPageLoadMetricsObserver, mark constants are made inline
constexpr and the milestone lookup is implemented using
base::MakeFixedFlatMap with std::string_view keys.

Revert CHECK in AXTreeCombiner::Combine to DCHECK

When trees_.size() == 1, root_tree_id_ may not match the tree_id of
trees_[0] under certain conditions in release builds, triggering a fatal
CHECK crash. Revert this CHECK back to a DCHECK to avoid crashes until
it is safe to enforce.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/8112947708400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

Bug: 580312549

media/v4l2: migrate MT8173 decoder limits to GPU workaround

Migrates MT8173 (ChromeOS Hana/Elm) decoder limits determination from a
V4L2 query to GPU workaround. There is already a workaround to limit the
number of decoders to 10 which works around an issue with AMD devices.

This should be a pure refactor, but it may change behavior on ChromeOS
due to the use of OOPVideoDecoder since it can now correctly limit the
number of decoders globally.

tree
7e8a726335717bf2a55cb48d282cd06ccfd1cc8e
parent
f6d3c9cc402c54952151861e27a5d3878fea329d [ diff ] 
ash: Fix SplitViewHighlightView border staying visible when hidden

Parent SplitViewHighlightView's SystemDecoration layer directly under
SplitViewHighlightView::layer() via SystemDecoration::Create() instead
of adding it as a sibling region layer via CreateForView().

SplitViewHighlightView controls visibility and animations by mutating
layer() opacity and clip_rect directly (e.g., setting opacity to 0.f
when hidden and animating clip_rect in SetBounds()). Parenting the
decoration layer under layer() ensures the highlight border inherits
both opacity and clip-rect animations, while AddedToWidget(),
RemovedFromWidget(), and OnBoundsChanged() keep its color provider
observation and local content bounds in sync.

tree
36941046b53e035fca1d2e55d6dc3a093d051904
parent
dbfa1fe64bcde3d6143cfd986c362cde6ca3d700 [ diff ] 
ash: Migrate HotseatWidget to use nine-patch highlight border

Migrate HotseatWidgetDelegateView to configure its highlight border via
SystemDecoration rather than using views::HighlightBorder.

SystemDecoration draws both the elevation 12 drop shadow and the
highlight border on a single nine-patch layer, eliminating the need for
CPU-side border painting on the translucent background view.

When the home launcher is shown in tablet mode, HotseatWidget clears the
shadow via decoration_->SetShadow(ShadowType::kNone) while keeping the
highlight border active; SystemDecoration automatically switches the
border colors between kHighlightBorderOnShadow and
kHighlightBorderNoShadow.

Since translucent_background_ no longer has a view border, back it with
ui::LAYER_SOLID_COLOR and set its color directly via the solid color
layer.

into CHECKs. The converted CHECKs are not fatal until M163.

Revert CHECK to DCHECK for degenerate range rect width in AXRange

The CHECK verifying that degenerate range rect width is zero can be
triggered in certain scenarios, leading to crashes. This change reverts
the CHECK back to a DCHECK with a TODO comment to convert it back once
we are confident it will not be triggered.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/dd9bcdb708400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

only existed to enable the feature, and rename the unit tests to
VisitedManifestManagerTest.

Migrate QuicSessionPool to base::MemoryConsumer
📋 Metadata
commit
2fa215152052e2a15cefc574f1aee63b715eb01c
[ log ]
author
Patrick Monette <pmonette@chromium.org>
Wed Nov 04 05:17:22 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Nov 04 05:17:22 2026
tree
e358559bb4de7fcabeefd307bc8b55e504391ae3
parent
c3722310328b5ee766a73abb0c5ce9930fdfa444 [ diff ] 
Migrate QuicSessionPool to base::MemoryConsumer

All base::MemoryPressureListener implementations are being migrated to
the new memory coordinator API. This new system gives policies much more
flexibility on how memory is managed across Chrome processes.

QuicCryptoClientConfigOwner is registered as a stateless consumer
(IsStateful::kNo): OnUpdateMemoryLimit() is a no-op, and
OnReleaseMemory() maps the memory limit back to the legacy pressure
levels. The kIgnoreQuicCryptoConfigMemoryPressure(ForDoh) logic is
unchanged, so there is no behavior change with default flags.

This also removes the stateful path added in crrev.com/c/7595975
(scaling the session cache size with the memory limit under
base::kStatefulMemoryPressure). It was only reachable on desktop when
kIgnoreQuicCryptoConfigMemoryPressure is disabled, and could shrink the
cache to zero entries under critical pressure. In that configuration,
the legacy behavior (clear on critical, evict expired entries on
moderate) now applies, matching WebView and the experiment's control
arm. Stateful support may be revisited in a follow-up.

tree
5e4482df2afe83c4321893c06ca8f751e760d079
parent
57635f1b7e9fb17e0b9d0aa9cb946614a26ba3ba [ diff ] 
[OSSPC] Disable the overlay on detecting hovers or TalkBack mode

The SuggestionsOmtOverlayView is designed to intercept only ACTION_DOWN
events from finger touches but currently interferes with TalkBack mode
which also intercepts input gestures and breaks other hover event
processing (such as from a stylus) for the Omnibox Suggestions dropdown.
See the first linked bug report. These types of events are not the
intended usecase so we disable the overlay completely when either
TalkBack mode is enabled or hover inputs are detected.

gpu: Share GL finalization helpers with GpuInit2::InitializeInProcess()
📋 Metadata

7551f1b5bd5bd8c9196938060aab0ac8da08643d

Wed Nov 04 05:02:13 2026
tree
5b474fc124377b7557ce29b0a69394c882713b86
parent
be0b5d8f3ca845b854ed58e13d6a1a4134f0c847 [ diff ] 
gpu: Share GL finalization helpers with GpuInit2::InitializeInProcess()

InitializeInProcess() repeated, inline, most of what the helpers used by
InitializeAndStartSandbox() do: collecting context info and recomputing
features (including the Windows WARP fallback), extension setup, the
default offscreen surface, extra info collection, the Linux core profile
recollection, the WARP info adjustment, disabling WebNN when GL is
disabled, and the native pixmap query.

Let FinalizeFeaturesWithContextInfo() and FinalizeGLInitialization()
handle the in-process case instead: failures are only logged and crash
keys are not set, as before. InitializeInProcess() now calls the helpers.

Also compute InitState::gl_disabled once in
InitializeGLBindingsAndDisplay(), and set
GPUInfo::gl_implementation_parts at the end of
FinalizeGLInitialization() for both paths.

Minor in-process behavior differences:
- CollectGpuExtraInfo() now runs before extension setup, and
  InitializePlatformOverlaySettings() runs after the WARP info
  adjustment, matching InitializeAndStartSandbox().
- gl_implementation_parts is set after a WARP reinitialization, so it
  reflects WARP instead of the hardware GL implementation.

[Vertical Tabs] Remove representative TAB_ID from header test models

Group header models in vertical tab tests previously populated TAB_ID
with representative member tab IDs, and keyboard navigation tests
stubbed getRelatedTabList lookups for those IDs.

Because vertical tabs resolves group identity and header actions by
Token, these representative tab IDs are unnecessary in test fixtures.
Removing them aligns the test models with Token-based group handling and
cleans up unused tab model stubs.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
Stop compositor animations in throttled frames
📋 Metadata
commit
e4fc207c587872ba2b22aaefec043acf0382e9cc
[ log ]
author
Hoch Hochkeppel <mhochk@microsoft.com>
Wed Nov 04 04:47:42 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Nov 04 04:47:42 2026
tree
c54238948b5d58c35b7e04ce353189f4b07ce299
parent
686d9a66e2a83bbaf12230c7c9acbaa4ec5bcf20 [ diff ] 
Stop compositor animations in throttled frames

When a child frame becomes render-throttled, its painted output is
omitted from the containing local root's property trees. The child's
lifecycle is also skipped, which allowed its compositor animations to
remain active after their required property nodes had been removed. This
left stale compositor animation state and violated the property-tree
invariant during subsequent animation synchronization.

This change cancels active compositor animations when their frame
becomes throttled and marks them for restart. This removes stale
compositor state before the property-tree rebuild while preserving the
Blink animation's running state and timeline. When the frame becomes
visible again, normal pending-animation processing recreates its
compositor animations with the restored paint properties.

Wire the Semantic Embedder origin trial

The Semantic Embedder API goes to an Origin Trial on Windows, macOS,
Linux and ChromeOS.

This CL makes five changes:

1. Give AIEmbeddingsAPI the "AISemanticEmbedderAPI" origin trial for
   Window contexts on Windows, macOS, Linux and ChromeOS, with
   third-party tokens allowed. Like the Writer API, its status is
   "experimental" on those four platforms and unset elsewhere, so
   chrome://flags#enable-experimental-web-platform-features also
   exposes it on desktop. AIEmbeddingsAPIForWorkers is unchanged, so a
   page's token does not expose SemanticEmbedder in its workers.
2. Set base_feature_status: "enabled", which turns
   blink::features::kAIEmbeddingsAPI on by default for the browser-side
   checks on trial pages, and copied_from_base_feature_if: "overridden",
   which keeps that default from exposing the API in Blink: a token or
   an explicit override is still required. The Writer, Rewriter and
   Proofreader trials use the same settings. Nothing is downloaded at
   startup for profiles that never called create(). Profiles that
   already set the download-eligibility pref, for example during the
   DevTrial, now register the component at startup without a flag.
3. Tie the trial to kAIEmbeddingsAPI in
   OriginTrialContext::CanEnableTrialFromName(), as for the other
   Built-in AI trials, so disabling the feature through Finch also
   stops tokens from exposing the API.
4. Remove SemanticEmbedder from the Android WebView interface listing.
   Android gets no status, and WebExposedTest checks that listing with
   the experimental and test features on. The desktop listings don't
   change, since web tests turn both on.
5. Cover SemanticEmbedder in ai_on_device_interactive_uitest like the
   other APIs in origin trial: flag, worker flag, kill switch and token.

Bug: 579902883
Test: interactive_ui_tests --gtest_filter=*AIOnDeviceBrowserTest*
Test: webview_instrumentation_test_apk WebExposedTest#testGlobalInterfaceListingUnstable (Android bots only)

c1405436c6c7bf4049cbdaab644c4d1dec7553cf [ diff ] 
mojo: Optimize generated interface Proxy, Responder, and PtrState

Reduce generated code size for C++ mojom interfaces:
- Mark local mojo::Message variables in generated Proxy and Responder
  methods as STACK_UNINITIALIZED to skip redundant stack pattern-init
  before the Message constructor runs.
- Collapse generated *_ProxyToResponder classes into static Run
  functions backed by a shared ProxyToResponder::CreateCallback helper.
- Avoid allocating PassThroughFilter instances when an interface's
  ResponseValidator_ is PassThroughFilter.
- Move SequencedTaskRunner by rvalue reference in BindingStateBase::Bind
  and AssociatedInterfacePtrStateBase::Bind, and mark
  ScopedInterfaceEndpointHandle constructors NOINLINE.

On Linux x64 official builds, this reduces chrome binary size by ~528 KB.

mojo: Factor MessageFragment allocation into MessageFragmentBase

Move MessageFragment allocation logic out of templated
MessageFragment<T> and MessageFragment<Array_Data<T>> methods into
non-templated methods on MessageFragmentBase (AllocateFragment,
AllocateUnversionedStructFragment, AllocateVersionedStructFragment) and
discrete FixedSizeArrayMessageFragment / BitPackedArrayMessageFragment
base classes (keeping NOINLINE on the high-fanout
AllocateUnversionedStructFragment and
FixedSizeArrayMessageFragment::AllocateArrayFragment definitions), and
default generated *_Data constructors in struct_declaration.tmpl,
union_declaration.tmpl, Array_Data, and Map_Data.

On Linux x64 official builds, this reduces chrome binary size by
~428 KB.

67d3e21d2e0f8d6db4550265f17ceba4721e0723 [ diff ] 
Add UMA metrics for image load event queue wait time.

Measure the main-thread task queue wait time between when an image
resource finishes loading (ImageLoader::ImageNotifyFinished) and when
its queued load event task starts executing on the main thread
(ImageLoader::DispatchPendingLoadEvent).

This CL records patterned UMA histograms
Blink.ImageLoader.LoadEventQueueWaitTime{SourceType} for all images,
data: URL images, and network images.

🌺 Revert "Reland "[TTC] Offer create_window and close_window on desktop windowing""

This reverts commit f77e39415b7dce2bb1b528a7fc606132d44dff1f.
Culprit: f77e39415b7d - "Reland "[TTC] Offer create_window and close_window on desktop windowing"" (refs/heads/main@{#1734499}, https://crrev.com/c/8718211)

Reason for revert: consistently fails android-11-x86-rel.

Mechanism: ToolControllerWindowToolsBrowserTest.CreateAndCloseWindow has
failed 3 consecutive builds on chromium/ci/android-11-x86-rel (#28912,
#28913, #28914, https://ci.chromium.org/b/8666103868269494385) starting
at #1734499. The reland skipped the test below SDK_VERSION_R (Android 11
/ API 30), but MultiWindowUtils.isMultiInstanceApi31Enabled() requires
API 31 (SDK_VERSION_S / Android 12), so android-11-x86-rel (API 30)
still runs the test and crashes with java.lang.AssertionError: Current
windowing mode doesn't support opening a new window in
MultiWindowUtils.createNewWindowIntent.

ToolControllerWindowToolsBrowserTest.CreateAndCloseWindow has failed in
3/25 recent builds of android-11-x86-rel, every attempt in every one of
them. This is not flake: it reproduces on every run, so it blocks the CQ
for every unrelated CL that touches this suite. The regression appears
at refs/heads/main@{#1734499}.

Example failing run:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/android-11-x86-rel/28914

To run/deflake this test on CQ before relanding, add the following
footer to your CL description:
  Include-Ci-Only-Tests: chromium.android:android-11-x86-rel|android_browsertests

Reverting to green the tree first. Please re-land once the failure is
understood and covered; this revert is about restoring CQ throughput,
not about the merit of the change.

Original change's description:
> Reland "[TTC] Offer create_window and close_window on desktop windowing"
>
> This is a reland of commit daea0cad7eaa2eaa5484c8e0e201b406e5a4ecde
>
> The original CL was reverted because
> ToolControllerWindowToolsBrowserTest.CreateAndCloseWindow crashed in
> android_browsertests on android-10-x86-rel. The test forces Android
> desktop mode so that TTC offers the window tools, and create_window
> then opens a second window. On Android that goes through
> MultiWindowUtils.createNewWindowIntent(), which asserts unless Chrome's
> multi-instance support is available, and isMultiInstanceApi31Enabled()
> is unconditionally false below Android R. The uncaught Java
> AssertionError ("Current windowing mode doesn't support opening a new
> window") took down the test process.
>
> Desktop mode only exists on much newer Android, so a desktop-mode
> device below R isn't a real configuration; the production check in
> SupportsWindowManagementTools() is unchanged. The fixture now skips on
> Android below R, as the actor's own window tool tests effectively do
> (they skip below S for Glic eligibility, which is why they didn't catch
> this).
>
> Patchset 1 is the original CL, unchanged.
>
> Original change's description:
> > [TTC] Offer create_window and close_window on desktop windowing
> >
> > TTC didn't expose any window management tools. Now that the actor's
> > CreateWindow and CloseWindow work on Android, offer them where Chrome
> > runs in a desktop-style windowing environment with multiple user-visible
> > windows: desktop platforms, and Android in desktop mode. On phones a
> > second window means split-screen, which isn't what a user asking for a
> > new window expects, so the tools are neither advertised nor dispatched
> > there.
> >
> > create_window comes from the actor ToolRegistry as-is. The new window
> > becomes active, and the session's tracked tab follows the active window
> > through VoiceFocusedContentsTracker, so subsequent tools act in the new
> > window without extra plumbing.
> >
> > close_window is defined by TTC rather than taken from the registry: the
> > registry definition takes a window_id parameter, but the model has no
> > window IDs. TTC closes the window containing the tracked tab, the same
> > way enter_fullscreen and exit_fullscreen resolve their window.
> >
> > activate_window is left for a follow-up since it needs a way for the
> > model to refer to a window.
> >
> > Bug: 470475787
> > Test: ToolControllerBrowserTest.* and ToolControllerWindowToolsBrowserTest.* (android_browsertests on android_36 emulator, browser_tests on Linux)
> > Change-Id: Ifb166f98e87852e39844c68f29b579c50b77f011
> > Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8695655
> > Reviewed-by: Miguel Ruiz <migueloruiz@google.com>
> > Commit-Queue: James Cook <jamescook@chromium.org>
> > Cr-Commit-Position: refs/heads/main@{#1734188}
>
> Bug: 470475787
> Test: ToolControllerWindowToolsBrowserTest.CreateAndCloseWindow and ToolControllerBrowserTest.GetToolDefinitions (android_browsertests on android_36 emulator); the skip path is covered by android-10-x86-rel via Include-Ci-Only-Tests

WebUIToolbar: Fix BrowserUserEducationContextUiTest

When WebUIToolbar is enabled, kToolbarAppMenuButtonElementId is hosted
in the WebUI toolbar as a TrackedElementWebUI rather than a native
Views TrackedElementViews. As a result, the View tests in
BrowserUserEducationContextUiTest did not exercise the
TrackedElementViews branch of GetDefaultElementFilter() (implemented by
BrowserUserEducationContext::Filter), and TwoProfilesDoesNotFindView
and TracksViewInActiveBrowser crashed in WithView when trying to hide
kToolbarAppMenuButtonElementId.

Update BrowserUserEducationContextUiTest to use kNewTabButtonElementId
instead of kToolbarAppMenuButtonElementId, as NewTabButton is always
present in tabbed browser windows and remains a native Views control in
both Views and WebUI toolbar configurations.

Fixed tests in BrowserUserEducationContextUiTest:
* TwoProfilesDoesNotFindView
* TracksViewInActiveBrowser

[History] Fix DST flake in GetDailyVisitsToOrigin_WithVisits

VisitDatabaseTest.GetDailyVisitsToOrigin_WithVisits assumes that
calendar days are always 24 hours. On Autumn Daylight Saving Time
transition days (e.g. the first Sunday of November, which is 25 hours
long in US timezones), adding 24 hours to day1_time lands at 23:00 on
the same calendar day instead of moving to the next day. This causes
all visits to collapse into a single day, failing the expectation of 2
days with visits.

Fix this by using the established pattern in
ExpireHistoryArgs::SetTimeRangeForOneDay of adding 36 hours and
rounding down to LocalMidnight() to safely step across days regardless
of DST transitions, and using (day2_time - base::Seconds(1)) as the
end of the first day.

Reserve capacity for AXTreeUpdate nodes in PdfAccessibilityTree

`update.nodes` was reported as one of the largest unreserved vectors in
the Chromium codebase. On large PDFs, peak size could exceed 100,000+,
and the reserve potentially saves 18 vector reallocations.

25179e320607bf86d5d96292fec0eb5f42ac6376

tree
4c382be9c942922ec9fdcd0823c38612a757b5ff
parent
f5fb3bb3ec24f9c09ab630d81b94a0f88e621ab8 [ diff ] 
views: Skip redundant ThemeChanged in Widget::SetNativeTheme

`Widget::SetNativeTheme()` previously ran a full `ThemeChanged()` pass
even when the widget's effective `ui::NativeTheme*` did not change (for
example, when `BrowserWidget::InitBrowserWidget()` explicitly sets the
native theme right after `Widget::Init()`).

This CL introduces `views::features::kSkipRedundantSetNativeThemeUpdate`
to skip `ThemeChanged()` when the effective `NativeTheme` is unchanged.
With the redundant post-`Widget::Init()` pass skipped, this CL also
configures the initial native theme before view attachment, updates
frame colors in `BrowserView::AddedToWidget()`, and initializes
`LocationBarView` content-setting icons with `kColorOmniboxActionIcon`.

Local Crossbench `browser-startup` A/B results (`google_startup`, N=32):
- `Startup.BrowserWindow.FirstPaint`: -10.50 ms / -1.81%
  (P50 (median) 581.5 -> 570.5 ms, 95% CI [-22.50, -2.00] ms)
- `InitialWebUI.Startup.ReloadButton.FirstContentfulPaint`: -12.00 ms /
  -1.97% (P50 (median) 609.0 -> 598.0 ms, 95% CI [-25.50, -2.00] ms)
- `Startup.FirstWebContents.NonEmptyPaint3`: -9.00 ms / -1.32%
  (P50 (median) 681.5 -> 668.0 ms, 95% CI [-23.51, -2.00] ms)

Bug: 40280130

98d2dc9f10410bd6460f9ddd365de23f3904e105 [ diff ] 
content: Stop 2190 test files from compiling frame.mojom-test-utils.h

In a ChromeOS Chrome build, 2322 test files include
browser_test_utils.h. With this change, 2190 of them no longer compile
the generated frame.mojom-test-utils.h, and 2070 of those no longer
compile frame.mojom.h either. On 50 of the 2070 files, picked at
random, compile time fell by 0.74 s per file (4.1% in total), 49 of 50
got faster, and peak memory fell 3.8%. Extrapolated to the 2070 files,
that is about 25 CPU-minutes per full build.

browser_test_utils.h included frame.mojom-test-utils.h only for two
popup helpers, CreateNewPopupWidgetInterceptor and
ShowPopupWidgetWaiter, which few tests use. This moves them, with the
related RequestCloseWidgetInterceptor, to the new popup_test_utils.h.

Test: autoninja (objects that include browser_test_utils.h, ChromeOS
and Linux builds); gn check

a3631b30524ec59338afb313a0178c9abeb96c3f [ diff ] 
login: Inject DeviceRestrictionScheduleController into LoginDisplayHost

Inject policy::DeviceRestrictionScheduleController* into
ash::LoginDisplayHostCommon and its subclasses (LoginDisplayHostMojo and
LoginDisplayHostWebUI), replacing g_browser_process->platform_part()
->device_restriction_schedule_controller() when constructing
WizardController in LoginDisplayHostMojo::EnsureOobeDialogLoaded(),
LoginDisplayHostWebUI::StartWizard() and
LoginDisplayHostWebUI::OnStartAppLaunch(). No g_browser_process use is
left in those three statements, so their TODO comments are removed.

Pass it from ShowLoginWizard() (through TriggerShowLoginWizardFinish(),
OnLanguageSwitchedCallback() and ShowLoginWizardFinish()),
SwitchWebUItoMojo() (from WizardController's existing
device_restriction_schedule_controller_, CHECKed first like
metrics_service_ because WizardController allows it to be null in unit
tests) and UserAddingScreenImpl::Start() (which still reads it from
g_browser_process under its existing TODO), and thread it from
ChromeBrowserMainPartsAsh::PostProfileInit() through
ChromeSessionManager::Initialize(), StartAutoLaunchKioskSession(),
StartKioskSession() and StartLoginOobeSession(). ChromeSessionManager
is constructed in PreCreateMainMessageLoop(), long before
PostProfileInit() creates the controller, so Initialize() takes it
rather than the constructor.

BrowserProcessPlatformPart creates the controller once, in
InitializeDeviceRestrictionScheduleController() called from
PostProfileInit() for the initial profile before
ChromeSessionManager::Initialize(), and destroys it in
ShutdownDeviceRestrictionScheduleController() called from
ChromeBrowserMainPartsAsh::PostMainMessageLoopRun() after the main
RunLoop exits. Every LoginDisplayHost is created at or after that
Initialize() call and destroyed inside the main RunLoop, because its
ScopedKeepAlive (KeepAliveRestartOption::DISABLED) keeps the RunLoop
from quitting while it exists. So the controller is non-null and
outlives every LoginDisplayHost, and WizardController receives the same
instance as before.

Dependency injection flow:
  ChromeBrowserMainPartsAsh::PostProfileInit
  +--> ChromeSessionManager::Initialize
       +--> StartAutoLaunchKioskSession
       |    +--> StartKioskSession
       |         +--> LoginDisplayHostWebUI
       +--> StartLoginOobeSession
            +--> ShowLoginWizard

  WizardController::OnEnrollmentDone / OnDemoSetupScreenExit
  +--> SwitchWebUItoMojo
       +--> ShowLoginWizard

  ShowLoginWizard
  +--> LoginDisplayHostWebUI
  +--> TriggerShowLoginWizardFinish
       +--> (OnLanguageSwitchedCallback)
            +--> ShowLoginWizardFinish
                 +--> LoginDisplayHostMojo / LoginDisplayHostWebUI

  UserAddingScreenImpl::Start
  +--> LoginDisplayHostMojo

  LoginDisplayHostMojo / LoginDisplayHostWebUI
  +--> LoginDisplayHostCommon (device_restriction_schedule_controller_)
  +--> WizardController (device_restriction_schedule_controller_)

TAG=agy

65f48ccd8dbd2a51af3b0fee11bed8c3fb38511a
parent
a4a389ea3bf009efff920c3c77e3fac751354f5f [ diff ] 
[fuchsia] Suppress video pixel test failures on Smart Display

Protected video buffers are enforced on Fuchsia Smart Display devices
(--force-protected-video-output-buffers), so hardware-decoded frames
cannot be read back by tab.Screenshot() and render as solid magenta
(DCHECK_IS_ON) or black.

For H.264 video tests, amlogic_video frequently hits decode errors on
four-colors.mp4, triggering an immediate fallback to software
FFmpegVideoDecoder (which uses unprotected memory and passes). This
flaky fallback caused unexpected_pass_finder.py to narrow the
expectations to Astro only, exposing Sherlock failures once the Skia
Gold bypass was lifted.

Mark these tests as [ Failure ] for [ angle-vulkan arm fuchsia
web-engine-shell ] and disable unexpected_pass_finder from removing
or narrowing them via finder:disable-general.

parent
3aff8d7b735d0e60970c08aee390eb1040574b97 [ diff ] 
WebUIToolbar: Fix RotatingPromoTest with WebUIToolbar

When `WebUIToolbar` (`WebUIAppMenuButton`) is enabled,
`kToolbarAppMenuButtonElementId` is registered as a
`ui::TrackedElementWebUI` rather than a `views::TrackedElementViews`.
`BrowserFeaturePromoControllerRotatingPromoTest::VerifyHasHelpBubble`
previously called `CheckView(id, ...)`, which assumed the element was a
`views::TrackedElementViews` and crashed with a null `views::View`
pointer when checking `kHasInProductHelpPromoKey` on the app menu
button.

When a floating `HelpBubbleViews` is anchored to a
`ui::TrackedElementWebUI`, `FloatingWebUIHelpBubbleFactory` anchors the
bubble to the hosting `views::WebView` and `HelpBubbleViews` sets
`kHasInProductHelpPromoKey` on that `views::WebView`. Update
`VerifyHasHelpBubble` to use `CheckElement(id, ...)` and inspect the
property on either the `views::TrackedElementViews` view or the
`ui::TrackedElementWebUI` hosting `views::WebView`.

Fixes the following `BrowserFeaturePromoControllerRotatingPromoTest`
tests with `WebUIToolbar` enabled:
* `SnoozeButtonRepeats`
* `TwoPromosRotating`

[dcheck-to-check] Convert DCHECKs in ui/ [4/23]

As part of effort to harden Chromium's safety, turn DCHECKs
into CHECKs. The converted CHECKs are not fatal until M163.

See also:

tree
7dbc2d4a113e78d093fdb467bc8248ac2b911c2d
parent
2b31a6e8528456a3d803c8cb9d4ad36fd2758006 [ diff ] 
Honor AbortSignal in SemanticEmbedder.embed()

SemanticEmbedderEmbedOptions always declared `signal`, but embed()
ignored it: an aborted call still ran the model and still resolved.

This CL makes five changes:

1. Reject embed() with the signal's reason when the signal is already
   aborted, before anything is sent. An abort while the call is in
   flight rejects the promise and sends
   AISemanticEmbedder.CancelEmbed(request_id) to the browser.
2. Give each Embed() a request id chosen by the renderer. It must be
   unique among the embedder's requests that have not been replied to
   yet, cancelled or not; reusing one is a bad message.
3. Keep the shared embedder's Job for each request in the browser until
   its reply. CancelEmbed() destroys the Job but keeps the id: a request
   the embedder has not started is dropped and replies null, and a
   started one finishes and the renderer ignores its reply. With the
   per-document pipe CL, CancelEmbed() sends CancelRequest() on the
   embedder's own pipe instead of destroying a Job, under the same
   contract.
4. Reject embed() with a QuotaExceededError while 32 calls are already
   in flight on the same embedder. A call counts until the browser
   replies, even after it was aborted. An in-flight embed() call now
   keeps its SemanticEmbedder alive (EmbedRequest holds it), so garbage
   collection can no longer close the embedder's pipe under a pending
   call.
5. Implement the new Embed() signature and CancelEmbed() in the
   content_shell echo embedder, and add a WPT for aborted embed()
   calls.

Bug: 579902094
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter='AISemanticEmbedderTest.*:AIManagerTest.*SemanticEmbedder*'
Test: blink_unittests --gtest_filter='EmbedderTest.*'
Test: run_web_tests.py external/wpt/ai/semantic_embedder

[PiP] Opt PiP windows out of window occlusion tracking

When window occlusion tracking is on, a video PiP window can stay
white while audio plays, and a Document PiP window freezes once the
browser window behind it is clicked.

WindowZOrderTracker ranks Chrome windows only by top resumed changes,
so when the browser window regains top resumed, it is ranked above the
PiP window even though PiP windows are pinned on top. If the browser
window covers the PiP window, WindowOcclusionTracker marks the PiP
window occluded, and since crrev.com/c/7867823 its compositor stops
drawing.

Fix this by:
- Not allowing occlusion tracking for VideoOverlayActivity and
  DocumentPictureInPictureActivity windows. Regular Chrome windows
  never cover a PiP window, so tracking it only produces false
  positives.

Teaching WindowZOrderTracker that PiP windows stay on top would give
them the same result (never occluded), but the tracker has no timely
signal: on desktop Android, PiP mode is reported about 0.5s after the
browser regains top resumed, and Document PiP's pinned layer is only
known to its own activity.

PiP windows also no longer count as occluders. They are small, so this
only gives up the saving for a Chrome window fully hidden under one.

  PropagateOcclusionToVizAndroid and
  VizBufferQueueDiscardOnVisibilityChange enabled, start video PiP with
  the browser maximized, and click the browser behind a Document PiP.
  Expect both PiP windows to keep rendering.

Test: On a desktop Android device with AndroidWindowOcclusion,

tree
d69315837c9c174c48e01aef7de42f0ae6743868
parent
3c4a6cddec334e1eeab40e80b1fb20bf0d06f054 [ diff ] 
PaymentRequestSpec: Adopt JniUniquePtr

PaymentRequestSpec stores its native
::payments::android::PaymentRequestSpec pointer in raw long field
mNativePointer. Passing raw integer handles across the JNI boundary
bypasses type checking and smart pointer lifecycle enforcement, risking
use-after-free bugs if the native object is accessed after destruction.

Migrate mNativePointer to JniUniquePtr with a package-private
JniTypeToken mapped to ::payments::android::PaymentRequestSpec via
@JniType. Return jni_zero::MakeUnique from the C++ factory, destroy the
owned instance via JniUniquePtr.destroy(), and pass JniPtr to
@NativeMethods calls.

2170ebc8260021b10f23042e7aca8ee814829942 [ diff ] 
[Glic] Use the tab URL origin for the page data of Android PDF tabs

Before (page data origin vs PDF data origin):
- Web PDF:   "" vs "https://example.com"
- Local PDF: "" vs ""

After:
- Web PDF:   "https://example.com" vs "https://example.com"
- Local PDF: "" vs "" (a local file has no website)

How: on Android, a PDF tab's page is an empty chrome-native://pdf
placeholder, which has no website, so its origin is empty (""). For PDF
tabs, use the origin of the URL shown in the omnibox
(WebContents::GetLastCommittedURL()) instead, which is what the PDF data
already uses. This matches desktop, where a PDF tab's page is at the
PDF's URL.

TAG=agy
CONV=4e98b4a0-1b61-4298-882b-92a10e60c5d6

1319fb7a514b5f226edaef5713d2db9bdb6298f7 [ diff ] 
[VerticalTabs] Don't activate tabs when dragging a collapsed group

Don't activate a tab in a collapsed tab group when dragging its header.
-Previously, dragging a collapsed group header activated the group's
 first tab without expanding the group, leaving a hidden tab active.
-Desktop treats this as a bug and no longer activates tabs in a
 collapsed dragged group. See https://crrev.com/c/8663683.
-Since the parent CL, TabCollectionTabModelImpl#setIndex also expands
 the group of a newly selected tab, so the drag would now expand the
 group instead.
-Now, dragging a collapsed group header leaves the active tab
 unchanged, matching desktop.

[TabStrip] Remove expand-on-select from StripLayoutHelper#tabSelected

Remove the collapsed group expansion from StripLayoutHelper#tabSelected.
-Previously, the strip expanded a collapsed group when one of its tabs
 was selected.
-This is now redundant. TabCollectionTabModelImpl#setIndex expands the
 group before notifying TabModelObserver#didSelectTab, which is the only
 path into StripLayoutHelper#tabSelected. See
 https://crrev.com/c/8691655.
-Also remove the now-unused TabSelectionType parameter from
 StripLayoutHelper#tabSelected.

Revert "Fix PiP occlusion false positives on Wayland"

This reverts commit e351d0881fba7d3576345a5e248810df77f9cbd4.

Reason for revert: Causes consistent failures in PictureInPictureOcclusionTrackerTest.* (8 tests) and PictureInPictureInputProtectorTest.BlocksKeyEventsWhenWidgetsOverlap in unit_tests, as well as FedCmAccountSelectionViewBrowserTest.DisabledWhenOccluded in browser_tests on linux-wayland-weston-rel-tests.

Failure Link: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/linux-wayland-weston-rel-tests/21237/overview

Original change's description:
> Fix PiP occlusion false positives on Wayland
>
> PictureInPictureOcclusionTracker compares the screen bounds of a
> security-sensitive widget (e.g. a permission prompt) with those of every
> picture-in-picture or floating widget. On platforms without global
> screen coordinates (Wayland), the position of top-level windows is
> unknown: document picture-in-picture windows report bounds at the origin
> and bubbles report bounds relative to their browser window. The
> intersection test then reports the prompt as occluded whenever a
> picture-in-picture window is open, so the prompt ignores all button
> presses. Google Meet opens a picture-in-picture window automatically on
> tab switch, which made location prompts in other tabs unusable.
>
> Only compare widgets that share a root widget on such platforms, since
> their bounds are relative to the same origin. Widgets in different
> top-level windows are treated as unoccluded.
>
> ExtensionInstallDialogPictureInPictureInputProtectorTest moves a
> picture-in-picture window over the extension install dialog and expects
> the dialog to be occluded. Update it to expect this only on platforms
> with global screen coordinates.
>
> Cq-Include-Trybots: luci.chromium.try:linux-wayland-mutter-rel
> Fixed: 525322795

5e6151ac3da2d7c902bc54b2ddb2e0ff937a7495 [ diff ] 
[Tabs] Expand collapsed tab group when selecting a tab in it

Expand a collapsed tab group when one of its tabs is selected.
-Groups opened from sync, the Tab Groups pane, or the archive start
 collapsed. Only StripLayoutHelper expands a group when a tab in it is
 selected, so on phones (no horizontal strip) these groups stay
 collapsed while the user browses them via the bottom tab strip.
-NextTabSelectionUtil and TabOpenerTrackerHelper skip collapsed groups,
 so closing the selected tab in such a group skipped the rest of the
 group and jumped to a tab in the nearest expanded group instead.
-Now, TabCollectionTabModelImpl#setIndex expands the selected tab's
 group. The selected tab is then never in a collapsed group, which
 matches desktop and iOS.

Bug: 563068664

[webapps] Add STE prose rules to harness doc templates

Before this change, agents authoring design documents and execution
plans defaulted to dense, agent-optimized prose, and DESIGNS.md lacked
guidance separating high-level architecture from detailed class sketches
and top-down diagram orientation.

Update harness-doc-writer/SKILL.md, DESIGNS.md, and PLANS.md to require
Simplified Technical English (ASD-STE100) for human-readable prose and
post-rewrite accuracy audits. Also update DESIGNS.md to focus Section 2
on architectural concepts and top-down diagrams while deferring class
sketches and caller gotchas to Section 8. The DESIGNS.md content mirrors
CL 8596376 PS5 (https://crrev.com/c/8596376) so that CL can drop the
file or rebase.

ba56b2f7f0aeeb368092b1f07fc1051e0b599e9c [ diff ] 
[Extensions] Expose webRequest.onErrorOccurred resolved remote IP

Previously, `details.ip` in `chrome.webRequest.onErrorOccurred` was only
populated if HTTP response headers were received before the failure.
When a request connected to a resolved remote IP and then timed out,
reset, or closed before headers arrived, `details.ip` was omitted.

This change notifies `TrustedURLLoaderHeaderClient` via
`OnLoaderFailedWithRemoteEndpoint()` from `URLLoader::OnResponseStarted`
on the error path before dispatching `URLLoaderClient::OnComplete()`,
and populates `WebRequestInfo::response_ip` on request errors.

Before/after screenshots and walkthrough:
https://crbug.com/41070013#comment28

Bug: 41070013
Test: services_unittests, unit_tests, browser_tests

tree
08feaa9628cb6e6997ee7200f63bd66d0bc8b45b
parent
0ae77683cedf6dfa4aa49946dea34abc4ced77ce [ diff ] 
[html-in-canvas] Track read-back disallowed state in PaintOps

This patch adds infrastructure for tracking read-back disallowed
content across `cc/paint`, which follow-up patches will use as a
defense-in-depth mitigation to ensure cross-origin image and frame
content is never revealed to canvas.

The high-level approach for PaintOps is to check whether the op itself
references read-back disallowed content (`*IgnoringFlags`) separately
from whether its flags do (`*FromFlags`). Entire recordings (e.g., from
cross-origin SVG images) can also be marked as disallowed with
`PaintRecord::SetIsReadBackDisallowed()` without the individual ops
being marked as disallowed.

This patch also adds a `strip_read_back_disallowed_ops` parameter to
`PaintOpBuffer::DeepCopyAsRecord()` and
`PaintRecord::ReplaceCustomData()` to omit disallowed paint ops,
balanced `SaveLayer[Filters]Op` scopes, and whole-record-disallowed
sub-records while filtering mixed sub-records.

tree
b1c5792cd0ced454e789fafdf6d87b1350186063
parent
8a7f5a2f79eb30d356f731980c66f19a0bbffcbe [ diff ] 
Don't expose nested pseudo-elements as snap event targets

PaintLayerScrollableArea::GetSnapEventTargetAlongAxis() retargeted a
pseudo-element snap target to its parentElement(). For nested
pseudo-elements such as ::column::scroll-marker, the parent is itself a
pseudo-element (::column), which was then exposed to script through
SnapEvent.snapTargetInline/snapTargetBlock. This is a variant of
crbug.com/570638836, where a pseudo-element leaked through the
LargestContentfulPaint API.

With a pseudo-element in hand, script could give it an id shared with
other elements and then run querySelector() with :dir(), which can
synchronously recalculate slot assignment and dispose the pseudo-element.
That clears the TreeOrderedMap entry's ordered list while
SelectorQuery::Execute() is iterating over it by reference.

Cq-Exclude-Trybots: chrome/try.security:vuln-scan-dispatcher

Allow BoringSSL to trigger Chromium try builds

BoringSSL would like to trigger Chromium try builds on a limited set of
chromium builders, on an opt-in basis:

    "android_compile_dbg",
    "android-arm64-rel",
    "linux-libfuzzer-asan-rel",
    "linux-rel",
    "mac_chromium_compile_dbg_ng",
    "win_chromium_compile_dbg_ng",

defined in BoringSSL's infra/config/main.star, and limited to users who
are allowed to trigger BoringSSL try jobs.

The hope is for these builders to be run on BoringSSL CLs that are more
likely to break Chromium, to help catch breakage earlier than the
autoroller.

fed05794b693b98647c3b74c4866a1c6f3b5e11f
parent
a068e66b63decf22cf4355cde3957504f8bfd301 [ diff ] 
gpu: Remove GPUInfo::max_msaa_samples

GPUInfo::max_msaa_samples is no longer consumed by any feature/workaround
decision logic, UMA metrics (GPU.MaxMSAASampleCount was removed in
crrev.com/c/5120557), or rendering pipeline, and is only populated when
creating a temporary GL context in CollectGraphicsInfoGL() (which is
skipped under kGpuInitOptimization and does not reflect non-GL rendering
backends such as Vulkan or Skia Graphite).

Remove max_msaa_samples from GPUInfo, its Mojo struct/traits,
CollectGraphicsInfoGL(), and chrome://gpu.

d01d9798aab8adbaf9f3654e109c0431dc2ab109 [ diff ] 
Prefetch: Remove embedder additional headers on cross-origin redirects

Prefetch kept the embedder's additional headers (e.g. those passed via
WebView's `AwPrefetchParameters`) across redirects, so a cross-origin
redirect sent them to an origin the embedder never targeted. Strip them
when a prefetch first follows a redirect to a different origin, as
`NavigationRequest::OnRedirectChecksComplete()` [1] does for
`WebView.navigate()`.

While `kPreloadingRespectUserAgentOverride` is enabled, `User-Agent` is
left out so that the overridden UA survives, since prefetch does not
re-apply WebContents overrides on redirects yet (crbug.com/441612842).
See crbug.com/511781454#comment15 for more details.

Guarded by `kPrefetchRedirectRemoveEmbedderAdditionalHeaders` (enabled
by default) as a kill switch. WebView end-to-end coverage is added
separately in crrev.com/c/8153563.

[1]
https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:content/browser/renderer_host/navigation_request.cc;l=6293-6314;drc=aff5d9bfe818c6c7ef7ad98615b46f9d04528595

parent
e7332a5510c8124d7735e76fe0c113b393153cd8 [ diff ] 
infra: Speed up android-binary-size-generator

* Use the chromium.android default for siso remote jobs (500) rather
  than 250. Builds that rebuild a large fraction of targets were
  bottlenecked on this limit: a 65 minute compile with ~52k remote
  actions spent an average of 14.6 minutes per action waiting for a
  local remote-exec slot, while RBE scheduling wait was ~5 seconds.
* Disable remote linking, matching try/android-binary-size. Remote
  ThinLTO links run on 8-core RBE workers and cannot use a persistent
  ThinLTO cache. libchrome.so (arm64) takes ~490s to link remotely even
  for small incremental builds, versus ~80-125s locally on the try
  builder, which has the same GN args and machine type.

[base] Store a feature's default state in its state pointer's low bits
📋 Metadata

e7332a5510c8124d7735e76fe0c113b393153cd8
[ log ]

tree
f8bdac191ecd96fd4ca1df73e405a143b0851dec
parent
186a7791bec43f3cf6b8bbc29583fd0a4f6fcd9b [ diff ] 
[base] Store a feature's default state in its state pointer's low bits

https://crrev.com/c/8601065 moved each feature's cache word into a
separate four-byte sidecar, and packed the default state into that word
so the descriptor could stay at two pointers. As a result, every
default-enabled or country-restricted feature has a non-zero initial
word and lands in .data rather than .bss.

Encode the default state in the low two bits of the descriptor's pointer
to its state storage instead. The storage is four-byte aligned, so these
bits are free, and pointing at byte N of the storage keeps the
descriptor constant-initialized. The tag is carried in the relocation
addend, so this adds no relocations.

The state word is now zero-initialized for all features except
runtime-mutable ones, which still set a flag bit. On ELF, these words
move from .data to .bss. Reading the default state no longer needs an
atomic load. Windows keeps .data$cr_feature_states, since clang emits
explicitly sectioned zero-initialized data as initialized data on COFF.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
[Webium] Disable the shared renderer in a process overflow test
📋 Metadata
commit
cfb79b0fdf264a9b76e45ec24e4850650b69fd27
[ log ]
author
Anna Sato <annasato@chromium.org>
Wed Nov 04 00:58:37 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Nov 04 00:58:37 2026
tree
609c71b4dc886448ecc75672a8be1f198ebb4422
parent
5fde32101c5317809c6ca4c54bef6c697721199e [ diff ] 
[Webium] Disable the shared renderer in a process overflow test

ProcessManagementTest.ExtensionAndWebProcessOverflow sets the process
limit relative to the renderer processes at startup. Since the testing
config enables the organizer panel (crrev.com/c/8672537), each window
creates its WebUI at startup in the profile of the window. With
kTopChromeWebUISharedRenderer, the WebUI toolbar is in
TopChromeWebUIProfile, so the organizer panel no longer shares its
renderer process. That adds a renderer process at startup, the limit no
longer leaves room for two processes for the same-site pages, and the
test fails.

Disable kTopChromeWebUISharedRenderer in ProcessManagementTest until
the organizer panel is also created in TopChromeWebUIProfile, so that
it shares the renderer process of the toolbar as it does without the
flag.

[Android] Deduplicate cached public deps

public_deps() caches flattened dependency lists, but those lists retain
one entry per dependency path. Repeated diamonds can therefore create
exponentially large intermediate lists before deps() removes duplicates.

Deduplicate each cached public closure in first-occurrence order, using
the same dict.fromkeys idiom as deps() and processor_deps(). Add tests
for repeated diamonds, exact ordering, and resource/group filtering.

On an upstream Android x64 release graph, window-javatests expands
1,565,192 entries for only 886 unique dependencies. Median generator
times across three alternating before/after pairs with Python 3.11.9:

The medians were 30.514s -> 0.175s for window-javatests, 8.658s ->
0.116s for chrome_java, and 27.278s -> 0.225s for chrome_junit.

All 14 generated metadata files, depfile inputs, and traversal sequences
were byte-identical across the pairs. Window-javatests peak worker RSS
fell from 166.4 MiB to 28.9 MiB. These are isolated generator
measurements, not whole-build speedups.

Test: params_json_util_test.py (3 tests); PRESUBMIT_test.py
Test: chrome_public_apk, chrome_public_test_apk, chrome_junit_tests

[remoting] Handle kUndefined in TransportRouteType FromMojom

mojom::TransportRouteType::kUndefined is a valid Mojom enum value
with no protocol::TransportRoute::RouteType equivalent. Because Mojo
wire validation allows kUndefined through to EnumTraits::FromMojom,
an uninitialized or malformed TransportRoute message breaks out of the
switch and hits NOTREACHED(), crashing the receiving daemon process.

Change FromMojom to return std::optional<TransportRoute::RouteType>
and return std::nullopt for kUndefined so Mojo rejects the message and
closes the IPC pipe instead of terminating the process.

Bug: None

[UI] Migrate ios/web/ to `ui::mojom::WindowOpenDisposition`

Spell the type as `ui::mojom::WindowOpenDisposition` instead of the
deprecated `WindowOpenDisposition` alias, and include
"ui/base/mojom/window_open_disposition.mojom-shared.h" instead of
"ui/base/window_open_disposition.h".

Headers that only use the type in declarations include the mojom
forward header instead.

This CL covers:
  ios/web/

No behavior change.

This CL was generated by running the following from src/, then
`git cl format`:

  migrate_window_open_disposition.py --migrate-type ios/web
  check_alias_includes.py --enumerators ios/web

By hand: add "//ui/base/mojom" to the targets whose files now include
the mojom header themselves (for `gn check`): public_deps of
//ios/web/content, whose content_web_state.h includes it, and deps of
//ios/web/web_state and //ios/web:ios_web_web_state_unittests. Also
rejoin a wrapped argument in crw_web_controller.mm that `git cl format`
left alone because the line itself did not change.

The scripts are attached to https://crbug.com/373551504.

[MSE] Replace broad TestExpectations suppressions with WPT baselines

Replace the blanket [ Failure Pass ] entries in TestExpectations for
external/wpt/media-source/ tests (originally added under crbug.com/807110
and crbug.com/715718) with explicit -expected.txt baselines now that all
builders running blink_web_tests and blink_wpt_tests build with
proprietary_codecs enabled.

This ensures passing subtests and specific failure messages are enforced
rather than ignoring all subtest results in these files.
